*2: When the system detects the CMBS failure while operating.

*7: When the system detects the image processing part failure in the multipurpose camera unit while operating.

*1: When the system detects the ACC failure while operating.

*2: When the system detects the CMBS failure while operating.

*3: When the system detects the LKAS failure while operating.

*4: When the system detects the RDM failure while operating.

*5: When the system detects a problem while operating, the system stops operating, then the system indicator and brake system indicator (amber) stay on.

*6: When the system detects the multipurpose camera unit software failure/multipurpose camera unit connector poor connections while operating.

*7: When the system detects the image processing part failure in the multipurpose camera unit while operating.
````
